## EPM:EPM-061

temporal splitting is not a ritual for unrelated claims.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Case/object dependency information, availability times, target definition, training/tuning isolation and a censoring policy.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/dc.md#r-dccfbb5471d14f250fb3ba8d576bf4af9c8b539a74c2a8a866e730f7c0221b1f">Complete shared assumptions · dccfbb5471d1</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Researcher states the claim and split design; reviewer examines what excluded cases and dependence do to the result. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange decision-time inputs, uncertainty, feasible actions, causal assumptions and observed actions/outcomes with the actual operational recipient. Specific receiving decision: Researcher states the claim and split design; reviewer examines what excluded cases and dependence do to the result.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Researcher states the claim and split design; reviewer examines what excluded cases and dependence do to the result.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The evaluation can be reconstructed and does not treat future outcomes or dependent prefixes as independent unseen evidence.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Repeated prefixes of one case cross train/test boundaries, or selection of completed cases makes long-running cases disappear from evaluation.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>A strict temporal split alone may still bias towards completed short cases. The longest-case trimming proposed in one benchmark is a population-changing heuristic, not a universal remedy.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/6f.md#r-6f95770452d8596f06f691dba98f0e8b440054a78a37e9a9441b2a8d5297220c">Complete shared criticism · 6f95770452d8</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S022">EPM:EPM-S022</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S023">EPM:EPM-S023</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-017.md#EPM-EPM-S056">EPM:EPM-S056</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R039</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R046</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R051</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R052</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>For an explicitly exchangeable retrospective question, a grouped random split can be acceptable; temporal splitting is not a ritual for unrelated claims.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. ## EPM:EPM-062

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-022.md#EPM-EPM-062">EPM:EPM-062</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-062</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Calibrated uncertainty and abstention</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM8</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S057</p></li><li><p>EPM-S058</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>DOCUMENTED_IMPORT_OR_HYBRID</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/98.md#r-98a66911cdecb345925189e0aa67ed80ee6f2e1959d988d49b19046ee0c301f3">Complete shared evidence limit · 98a66911cdec</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Prediction occasions, available features, calibration and predictive evaluation/utility</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>Outputs distinguish probability calibration, marginal set coverage and individual uncertainty; unreliable regimes trigger qualified output or review.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Choose uncertainty measures appropriate to the decision, evaluate calibration/coverage and set usefulness separately, and provide a human-review or abstention path where uncertainty defeats actionability. State assumptions such as exchangeability, valid calibration separation and limits under drift.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Prediction uncertainty materially affects the cost or safety of a decision and a consumer can use an uncertainty-aware output.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>A conservative rule, broad interval or human review may be preferable to a complex calibration layer whose assumptions cannot be justified.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Suitable calibration data, an explicit error/coverage target, temporal validity and an available response to abstention.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/fb.md#r-fb455bb210b6634cfbb7c25ef3541bec9957019139feab6cc441e0d36a6c4070">Complete shared assumptions · fb455bb210b6</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst explains uncertainty semantics; accountable operators decide how uncertain cases are handled rather than reading a singleton set as certainty. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/be.md#r-be688aa94abe419df16b545d6cc3e96535c48e87cc379fc47dfdc6e4d93c6171">Complete shared communication interaction · be688aa94abe</a></p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst explains uncertainty semantics; accountable operators decide how uncertain cases are handled rather than reading a singleton set as certainty.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>Outputs distinguish probability calibration, marginal set coverage and individual uncertainty; unreliable regimes trigger qualified output or review.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A high-confidence prediction is treated as reliable for a particular case, or conformal coverage is confused with calibrated probabilities and causal certainty.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>The sepsis study reuses one modest static dataset and disclaims deployment readiness. Conformal marginal coverage does not require accurate base probabilities, and it does not establish per-case conditional safety under arbitrary drift.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/6d.md#r-6dfbe342004589261fa865ff4785bc6dfcb52cfd3f64ea0250815cbb73c6e219">Complete shared criticism · 6dfbe3420045</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S022">EPM:EPM-S022</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-017.md#EPM-EPM-S057">EPM:EPM-S057</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-017.md#EPM-EPM-S058">EPM:EPM-S058</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R047</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R051</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>A conservative rule, broad interval or human review may be preferable to a complex calibration layer whose assumptions cannot be justified.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. id</dt><dd><p>EVOLVED_PROCESS_MINING</p></dd><dt>Candidate origin</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM8</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S023</p></li><li><p>EPM-S030</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Historical origin</dt><dd><p>Outcome monitoring extended to prescriptive decisions under limited intervention resources.</p></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Original form</dt><dd><p>Outcome monitoring extended to prescriptive decisions under limited intervention resources. The reconstructed initial problem is: A predictor improves a benchmark score after the useful intervention window has closed or recommends more actions than resources permit.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>A predictor improves a benchmark score after the useful intervention window has closed or recommends more actions than resources permit.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>A predictor improves a benchmark score after the useful intervention window has closed or recommends more actions than resources permit.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Evaluate predictive utility at actual decision times, accounting for lead time, false alarms, intervention capacity and opportunity cost. Choose thresholds or policies against an explicit decision objective rather than accuracy alone.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>Predictions are intended to allocate attention, resources or interventions to running cases.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>A simple prioritisation rule may be adequate, or no prediction is warranted if no timely action is possible.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p>A feasible action set, costs, resource constraints, decision times and an explicit baseline policy.</p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/7e.md#r-7eaa0f8e0a416ad25a582a960ccfdd7021002a0bddc55c8fff0265b3b1ea92c1">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · 7eaa0f8e0a41</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>A claimed useful prediction has an available response and an evaluation matched to its timing and resource constraints.</p></dd><dt>Status</dt><dd><p>EXPECTED_OR_LOGICALLY_REQUIRED_FOR_THE_STATED_CLAIM_NOT_A_UNIVERSAL_MEASURED_BENEFIT</p></dd><dt>Cost boundary</dt><dd><p>A simple prioritisation rule may be adequate, or no prediction is warranted if no timely action is possible.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Operational authority specifies available actions and resource limits; the analyst evaluates decision support within those bounds.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>Operational authority specifies available actions and resource limits; the analyst evaluates decision support within those bounds. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Exchange decision-time inputs, uncertainty, feasible actions, causal assumptions and observed actions/outcomes with the actual operational recipient. Specific receiving decision: Operational authority specifies available actions and resource limits; the analyst evaluates decision support within those bounds.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>A claimed useful prediction has an available response and an evaluation matched to its timing and resource constraints.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><p>Decision utility estimates may rely on unverified causal effects. Resource competition couples cases and can invalidate independent-case optimisation.</p></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/4b.md#r-4bc238f078125ed7be5d2eccedb6a015edbd60816ac3e9cae464a5af60d93cfc">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · 4bc238f07812</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><dl><dt>Transition</dt><dd><p>REFINED</p></dd><dt>Account</dt><dd><p>Refine predictive accuracy into conditional operational usefulness without assuming that more automated action is better.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Evaluate predictive utility at actual decision times, accounting for lead time, false alarms, intervention capacity and opportunity cost. Use experimental or suitably justified causal evidence for interventional claims, and otherwise present recommendations as hypotheses requiring evaluation.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A result claims that changing an activity, priority, resource or decision will improve an outcome.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Use prediction for preparation or communicate a descriptive association without claiming a causal redesign benefit.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>A feasible intervention, defensible causal structure/identification strategy, relevant confounders and adequate support for compared actions.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/6c.md#r-6c3a952849376cf3802129091590a3e04b05ec2797721b1e7be3d1938a9507b7">Complete shared assumptions · 6c3a95284937</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst establishes the causal claim’s conditions; authorised decision makers decide whether a test or intervention is acceptable. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange decision-time inputs, uncertainty, feasible actions, causal assumptions and observed actions/outcomes with the actual operational recipient. Specific receiving decision: Analyst establishes the causal claim’s conditions; authorised decision makers decide whether a test or intervention is acceptable.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst establishes the causal claim’s conditions; authorised decision makers decide whether a test or intervention is acceptable.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>An interventional claim states why the observational or experimental evidence identifies the proposed effect, and where it does not.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>The association between an action and a favourable outcome is treated as evidence that recommending the action will improve the outcome.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Sequential decisions, unmeasured confounding and inter-case interference can violate identification. Simulation or estimated treatment effects do not themselves establish real organisational benefit.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/f7.md#r-f7113f61502a1eb8a5c659604785ad08427dd4c53a66658916352fa8f8fb8934">Complete shared criticism · f7113f61502a</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S027">EPM:EPM-S027</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S030">EPM:EPM-S030</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R019</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R037</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R049</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R051</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R070</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Use prediction for preparation or communicate a descriptive association without claiming a causal redesign benefit.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. The reconstructed initial problem is: An optimised recommendation names an impossible, unauthorised or harmful action despite a favourable estimated outcome.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>An optimised recommendation names an impossible, unauthorised or harmful action despite a favourable estimated outcome.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>An optimised recommendation names an impossible, unauthorised or harmful action despite a favourable estimated outcome.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Restrict recommendations to actions that the recipient can perform within actual authority, timing and resource constraints; expose uncertainty and alternatives, allow rejection, and observe the action actually taken rather than equating advice with execution.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>A system offers case-level or process-level advice intended to change behaviour.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>Present a small set of options or a human-reviewed hypothesis rather than automatic execution.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p>An explicit action space, actor capability and authority, intervention timing, resource availability and consequence monitoring.</p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/6d.md#r-6d8236746586b1c6ab7c3bb9233d4f34567e6426f22ee593f7e549e271e108a5">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · 6d8236746586</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>Advice states its recipient, feasibility conditions and evaluation basis; acceptance and actual execution are distinguishable.</p></dd><dt>Status</dt><dd><p>EXPECTED_OR_LOGICALLY_REQUIRED_FOR_THE_STATED_CLAIM_NOT_A_UNIVERSAL_MEASURED_BENEFIT</p></dd><dt>Cost boundary</dt><dd><p>Present a small set of options or a human-reviewed hypothesis rather than automatic execution.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Operational actors retain the authority specified by the setting; ## EPM:EPM-066

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-022.md#EPM-EPM-066">EPM:EPM-066</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-066</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Evaluation renewal after intervention feedback</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM8</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S027</p></li><li><p>EPM-S030</p></li><li><p>EPM-S037</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/71.md#r-7152e4447466e1f3140a22d3cc330d37ea01196cde969e67f112dc10842c8f8c">Complete shared evidence limit · 7152e4447466</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Intervention identification, recommendation feasibility and post-intervention/deployment claims</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>Post-intervention claims cite evidence from the relevant regime and do not recycle an invalidated pre-intervention evaluation.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Record when recommendations or interventions alter exposure, action selection or outcomes; reassess predictive, calibration and causal claims against the new regime. Preserve the prior evaluation as historical evidence and explicitly test the revised policy or narrow its use.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Advice is acted upon, a policy is deployed, or user adaptation materially changes the process that produced training data.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>For unused exploratory predictions, no intervention-driven re-evaluation is needed beyond ordinary temporal checks.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Intervention and policy version identity, action/outcome observation, a comparison design and a consumer able to revise or suspend use.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/15.md#r-15d9c710e8ee07b0b01cabb948e31ad01e1226436e70b6927f9ceac1f26af6d9">Complete shared assumptions · 15d9c710e8ee</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst identifies changed validity conditions; operational authority decides continued use, revision or retirement based on new evidence. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Exchange decision-time inputs, uncertainty, feasible actions, causal assumptions and observed actions/outcomes with the actual operational recipient. Specific receiving decision: Analyst identifies changed validity conditions; operational authority decides continued use, revision or retirement based on new evidence.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst identifies changed validity conditions; operational authority decides continued use, revision or retirement based on new evidence.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>Post-intervention claims cite evidence from the relevant regime and do not recycle an invalidated pre-intervention evaluation.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A policy changes the process while its old benchmark score continues to be reported as if the data-generating regime were unchanged.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Re-evaluation itself is costly; withholding interventions for comparison may be unacceptable. Changed selection makes naive before/after comparisons confounded.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/88.md#r-887e3e2bc2f2079a0e40f38db1f27824cdb06da6552d7fe6cc1a95093aefd64f">Complete shared criticism · 887e3e2bc2f2</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S022">EPM:EPM-S022</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S027">EPM:EPM-S027</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-014.md#EPM-EPM-S030">EPM:EPM-S030</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-015.md#EPM-EPM-S037">EPM:EPM-S037</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R039</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R043</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R051</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>For unused exploratory predictions, no intervention-driven re-evaluation is needed beyond ordinary temporal checks.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><p>Reliable counterfactual evaluation under policy feedback and interference remains a research and deployment burden.</p></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/ab.md#r-abd1a48918bcf1c22ebb26f8fbf92feef45eb2b884e988fc8d40dcc24c583b12">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · abd1a48918bc</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Compose recommendation with feedback-sensitive revalidation, not perpetual reliance on a static benchmark. constrain an overclaim rather than create an obligation?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Native record</dt><dd><dl><dt>Property id</dt><dd><p>EPM-066</p></dd><dt>Property name</dt><dd><p>Evaluation renewal after intervention feedback</p></dd><dt>Lineage id</dt><dd><p>EVOLVED_PROCESS_MINING</p></dd><dt>Candidate origin</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM8</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S027</p></li><li><p>EPM-S030</p></li><li><p>EPM-S037</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Historical origin</dt><dd><p>Process adaptation and sequential intervention research expose feedback between recommendations, behaviour and later data.</p></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Original form</dt><dd><p>Process adaptation and sequential intervention research expose feedback between recommendations, behaviour and later data. The reconstructed initial problem is: A policy changes the process while its old benchmark score continues to be reported as if the data-generating regime were unchanged.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>A policy changes the process while its old benchmark score continues to be reported as if the data-generating regime were unchanged.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>A policy changes the process while its old benchmark score continues to be reported as if the data-generating regime were unchanged.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Record when recommendations or interventions alter exposure, action selection or outcomes; reassess predictive, calibration and causal claims against the new regime. Preserve the prior evaluation as historical evidence and explicitly test the revised policy or narrow its use.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>Advice is acted upon, a policy is deployed, or user adaptation materially changes the process that produced training data.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>For unused exploratory predictions, no intervention-driven re-evaluation is needed beyond ordinary temporal checks.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p>Intervention and policy version identity, action/outcome observation, a comparison design and a consumer able to revise or suspend use.</p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/ab.md#r-abd1a48918bcf1c22ebb26f8fbf92feef45eb2b884e988fc8d40dcc24c583b12">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · abd1a48918bc</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>Post-intervention claims cite evidence from the relevant regime and do not recycle an invalidated pre-intervention evaluation.</p></dd><dt>Status</dt><dd><p>EXPECTED_OR_LOGICALLY_REQUIRED_FOR_THE_STATED_CLAIM_NOT_A_UNIVERSAL_MEASURED_BENEFIT</p></dd><dt>Cost boundary</dt><dd><p>For unused exploratory predictions, no intervention-driven re-evaluation is needed beyond ordinary temporal checks.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst identifies changed validity conditions; operational authority decides continued use, revision or retirement based on new evidence.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>Analyst identifies changed validity conditions; operational authority decides continued use, revision or retirement based on new evidence. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Exchange decision-time inputs, uncertainty, feasible actions, causal assumptions and observed actions/outcomes with the actual operational recipient. Specific receiving decision: Analyst identifies changed validity conditions; operational authority decides continued use, revision or retirement based on new evidence.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>Post-intervention claims cite evidence from the relevant regime and do not recycle an invalidated pre-intervention evaluation.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><p>Re-evaluation itself is costly; withholding interventions for comparison may be unacceptable. Changed selection makes naive before/after comparisons confounded.</p></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/88.md#r-887e3e2bc2f2079a0e40f38db1f27824cdb06da6552d7fe6cc1a95093aefd64f">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · 887e3e2bc2f2</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><dl><dt>Transition</dt><dd><p>REFINED</p></dd><dt>Account</dt><dd><p>Compose recommendation with feedback-sensitive revalidation, not perpetual reliance on a static benchmark.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Record when recommendations or interventions alter exposure, action selection or outcomes; reassess predictive, calibration and causal claims against the new regime. <dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-022.md#EPM-EPM-069">EPM:EPM-069</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-069</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Reproducible executable analysis</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM9</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S036</p></li><li><p>EPM-S019</p></li><li><p>EPM-S056</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/0b.md#r-0b90f52f862a80d5585ff8d67ba0e9c4f4b52172b428b2fe79f6fff0f422187f">Complete shared evidence limit · 0b90f52f862a</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Analytical recipes, alternatives, evidence dependencies and validity-preserving handoffs</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>A competent reviewer can reproduce the declared computation or locate the exact blocked dependency.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Preserve the executable transformation/analysis recipe, relevant data identity, parameters, tool and dependency versions, random seeds and excluded or failed runs. <a id="EPM-EPM-070"></a>
## EPM:EPM-070

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-022.md#EPM-EPM-070">EPM:EPM-070</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EPM-070</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Robustness to plausible analytical alternatives</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>SOURCE_GROUNDED_MECHANISM_EXTRACTION</p></dd><dt>Family</dt><dd><p>PM9</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM-S019</p></li><li><p>EPM-S020</p></li><li><p>EPM-S053</p></li><li><p>EPM-S056</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>Registered during the scoped research; unfavourable and duplicate candidates remain in the denominator.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_PROCESS_MINING_CONTRIBUTION_OR_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>STRONGLY_RETAINED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/aa.md#r-aa899e44da77598cc27e34389bcdb9c4a5928b1b360cb492a33a604903cf1d8a">Complete shared evidence limit · aa899e44da77</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Analytical recipes, alternatives, evidence dependencies and validity-preserving handoffs</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>The finding states which reasonable changes preserve it and which would reverse or weaken it.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Vary plausible consequential analytical choices and identify which findings survive; treat changed conclusions as evidence about assumption sensitivity rather than selecting the preferred run. Include simple baselines and report computational failures.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Reasonable alternative extraction, representation, filtering, ordering, threshold or metric choices could change the decision.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Test a small set of material alternatives, not an exhaustive Cartesian product of every parameter.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>An explicit set of plausible alternatives and a criterion for material change; access to repeatable computation.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/95.md#r-9513183414c00f69254fb084e8cd7e7e74715773d992d63b387ed341d56e92bb">Complete shared assumptions · 9513183414c0</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Analyst defines and reports sensitivity; reviewer challenges whether excluded alternatives were genuinely implausible. Analytical competence does not itself supply additional organisational authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Provide reviewers and participants with a usable, lawful analytical chain, relevant sensitivity results, data dependence and unresolved assumptions. Specific receiving decision: Analyst defines and reports sensitivity; reviewer challenges whether excluded alternatives were genuinely implausible.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Analyst defines and reports sensitivity; reviewer challenges whether excluded alternatives were genuinely implausible.</p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The finding states which reasonable changes preserve it and which would reverse or weaken it.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>A conclusion depends on one arbitrary filter, abstraction, metric or seed but is reported as stable process knowledge.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Stability is not validity: a flower model can be stable and useless. Robustness checks can become expensive or selectively reported.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/ae.md#r-ae6280cf0054cf3e13c9c03a46eb888d9182d188a8dbc5fd53c2be7f2983623e">Complete shared criticism · ae6280cf0054</a></p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S018">EPM:EPM-S018</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S019">EPM:EPM-S019</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-013.md#EPM-EPM-S020">EPM:EPM-S020</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-017.md#EPM-EPM-S053">EPM:EPM-S053</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-017.md#EPM-EPM-S056">EPM:EPM-S056</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-017.md#EPM-EPM-S060">EPM:EPM-S060</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EPM:EPM-R054</p></li><li><p>EPM:EPM-R055</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Test a small set of material alternatives, not an exhaustive Cartesian product of every parameter.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. A justified existing substitute can supply the same function. Current native failure/authority/retention limits continue to apply.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Unresolved questions</dt><dd><p>Unconsidered semantic alternatives may dominate the measured parameter sensitivity.</p></dd><dt>Domain lifecycle scope</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/50.md#r-50b04344b446c5aa376584039ad24f6475678ed6ae0d52aa5f7e4e54381f8c50">Complete shared domain lifecycle scope · 50b04344b446</a></p></dd><dt>Disposition rationale</dt><dd><p>Refine sensitivity testing into a decision-relevant challenge rather than indiscriminate parameter sweeps. Stability is not validity: a flower model can be stable and useless. Robustness checks can become expensive or selectively reported.</p></dd><dt>Original namespace key</dt><dd><p>null</p></dd><dt>Original
